# Original Xbox and Xbox 360 ecosystem research — 2026-10-25

Owner: [#2931](https://github.com/boburning/portcove/issues/2931). Baseline main: `8ac0a9c1dfd906ef98798a8b5e3e9bc52fdc59e8`. This is a source-bound research handoff to the intake owner. It does not authorize or claim runtime qualification, publication, or catalog support.

## Current handoff and supersession

The **final v3 semantic review is complete for all 986 retained repository leads**. There is no unread 767-lead continuation remaining. `xbox/full-disposition-register-v3.json` is frozen at SHA-256 `edbd8bd3d1db9febf4cae5896f08135c9ebc13f68325f57b4658ec2d2c924954`. It preserves 128 prospective ownership/listing flags and two withheld rows; eligibility does not establish uniqueness, public creation, managed acquisition or qualification. The 301 research/watch rows in the full register are not an instruction to create 301 public port issues.

## Snapshot and coverage

The baseline includes 1,257 open/closed issue records (860 `[Port]` records), 1,257 archive-inclusive Project items, authored listings, operational definitions, and active PRs captured by the parent workstream. This researcher performed no GitHub issue/Project/catalog/artwork mutations. All evidence clocks are UTC on 2026-10-25; that evening began on October 24 in America/New_York.

The retained Xbox/XBE/ReXGlue/index lexical corpus began with 941 repositories. Cross-partition, direct downstream and focused Xenon/topic follow-through expanded the first handoff to 985; the directly reviewed XenonRecomp framework makes the final union 986. The final semantic dispositions are 246 Alias/helper, 125 Outside scope, 103 Existing canonical owner, 179 New canonical intake candidates, 301 Research/watch, one mixed game/lineage record, six Source hold, three rejected emulator-backed records and 22 genuinely unavailable retained README requests. Candidate dispositions remain subject to root canonical reconciliation and later identity amendments.

The earlier checkpoint of 94 interpreted repositories, 102 exact-marker overlaps and **767 pending interpretations was historical and is superseded** by the completed v3 register and final checkpoint below. Historical handoff files remain evidence; their partial counts are not current coverage. Completion concerns this frozen corpus, not all Internet projects or hands-on runtime qualification.

The original research used 321 bounded HTTP/API requests, all returning200, including144 continuation requests after the initial177-call checkpoint. The later identity questions used16 targeted calls:14 successes and two cross-repository comparison404s, preserved without retries. The 22 initial README404s remain parent-capture failures; neither a missing README nor compare404 establishes project deletion or alias identity. Requests retained20-second/2-MiB bounds; no upstream code was executed.

Frozen machine-readable handoffs are under `E:/Codex-Evidence/non-switch-ecosystems-20261024/xbox/`:

## Existing ownership that must survive this pass

The exact marker inventory preserves Modern Warfare 2 #1397; Dead Rising 2 Case Zero #1121 and Case West #1122; Eternal Sonata #1126; Lost Odyssey #744; Midnight Club Los Angeles #2693; The Simpsons Game #379; Saints Row #2778; Lollipop Chainsaw #162; Forza Motorsport 4 #2676; Gears of War: Judgment #2859; Sonic Free Riders #1450; and Kameo RePowered #129. These game names also occur in many new platform forks, which is not new-implementation evidence.

`existing-owners-v1.json` maps all 102 exact-marker repository hits to issue state and baseline listing IDs. Incidental framework links in issue bodies were not treated as canonical ownership. An issue being closed/deferred does not free its identity for recreation.

## Identity findings

- Auremana Broken Bond V2 explicitly forks Jorge Barbosa’s runtime and adds PC features. Attach it as a downstream variant rather than manufacturing an independent implementation from the new UI or release channel. DIMASIK1502 and TrinityForever15 have separate title-side projects and remain distinct until source lineage proves continuation.
- Tekken 6 Native Online’s release repository and `Ekspym/tekken6-native-online-source` are one project. It credits mahmoud-mess’s faster generated code; that reuse alone does not decide whether its runtime is independently maintained. Compare the native runtime before a separate canonical write. The Linux-only tutifrutas repository also needs this lineage check.
- MaxDeadBear/KameoRePowered explicitly points both releases and clone instructions to birabittoh/KameoRePowered: preserve #129. Azkali’s project is a separate stubbed bring-up. ElGus’s native renderer/importer requires lineage confirmation rather than game-name merging.
- apexJCL/burnout3-recomp explicitly preserves sp00nznet/burnout3 history. The build wizard, Metal backend and Proton route remain the same implementation family. MANXBurnout3 explicitly declares an independent MANXFramework runtime; its historical Windows prose must not overwrite the current Linux architecture. Existing Takedawn #2105 remains another implementation.
- Three Table Tennis projects have materially different source/setup/runtime routes. Keep candidate identities separate while comparing source lineage; never reduce them to one owner merely because all run the same retail title.
- `divyprj/tekken-tag-tournament-2-pc-port` explicitly calls itself a distribution wrapper around Shihab Al-Amri’s engine. `DevZer0D4Y/SonicFreeRiders-iOS` and DAODroid explicitly name their upstream implementations. These are strong duplicate-prevention fixtures.
- `VinceIP/fci-recomp` has two independently catalogable games, Instincts and Evolution, with different title IDs and executable versions. They share one implementation repository and build owner, not one undifferentiated game record.

## Framework opportunities

### sp00nznet/xboxrecomp

Original Xbox x86-to-C lifter and kernel/GPU/audio/input libraries; generates no game executable by itself. XBE analysis/function/ABI data, user disc extraction, generated C and game CMake project repeat. Title hooks/function fixes, assets and gameplay remain game-specific. Windows/Linux/macOS toolkit support does not establish every game host.

Source: [upstream primary README](https://github.com/sp00nznet/xboxrecomp/blob/main/README.md) (blob `4a05fc665cc68ff5bbb90e31444ef69ff76d7773`, observed `2026-10-25T03:56:05.179Z`).

### TGP482/XboxXBERecomp

Independent original Xbox x86-to-C++ toolchain/native D3D12 API implementation. Per-game config sets XBE/EXE and XDK version/signatures; unknown signatures trap, hooks remain title-specific. Releases can bundle lifter/Clang/LLD and locally generate game DLL from own XBE. Model one upstream family, not a universal promise that arbitrary titles work.

Source: [upstream primary README](https://github.com/TGP482/XboxXBERecomp/blob/main/README.md) (blob `751e9f00dddcbb5bb0b9377e80e44fa150aadfb8`, observed `2026-10-25T04:02:30.013Z`).

### rexglue/rexglue-sdk

Xbox360 static PowerPC-to-C++ codegen plus Xenia-derived console services and GPU runtime. Title-specific manifests/function fixes/runtime patches and native-renderer profiles remain. Local-codegen installers and prebuilt runtime-plus-own-data are materially different acquisition routes; respect upstream replacement/update ownership.

Source: [upstream primary README](https://github.com/rexglue/rexglue-sdk/blob/main/README.md) (blob `9e5b57e56279cd6043a9bd1b0fbffd5b854e9064`, observed `2026-10-25T04:00:19.771Z`).

### hedge-dev/XenonRecomp

Converter only, explicitly no runtime. Native game needs kernel/GPU/audio/input runtime; generated C++ success is not game support. Function/jump-table analysis and exception/MMIO limitations remain title-specific. Do not offer raw XenonRecomp as a player installer.

Source: [upstream primary README](https://github.com/hedge-dev/XenonRecomp/blob/main/README.md) (blob `756a0bbd6e562c36e823f00844d280f6f623d036`, observed `2026-10-25T04:16:00.826889+00:00`).

### apexJCL/xboxrecomp-cli

Repeated manifest/pins/import/fingerprint/analyze/recomp/build/package/wizard workflow is demonstrated by BLiNX2 and Burnout3. Game TOML configs and toolkit fork pins provide an authoring seam; first-launch/local build wizards already upstream-owned. SteamOS Windows build via Proton is not nativeLinux. Deterministic build outputs have not been reproduced by Portcove.

Source: [upstream primary README](https://github.com/apexJCL/xboxrecomp-cli/blob/main/README.md) (blob `d04d7438e7a23682ca5bd11388910ed822b93ee9`, observed `2026-10-25T04:01:46.987Z`).

### GloriousEggroll/squarehole360

Shared native Vulkan renderer/SDK fork with Gears3 concrete target and per-game executable/render profiles. Renderer library is infrastructure; each additional game needs demonstrated profile and runtime work. Do not create one synthetic port for allXbox360 or per-helper game issues.

Source: [upstream primary README](https://github.com/GloriousEggroll/squarehole360/blob/main/README.md) (blob `67475e26368689670278ac73921d6e24a800be3f`, observed `2026-10-25T03:58:44.600Z`).

### Recommended common authoring seam

Several actual games repeat a supported upstream route: verify a user-owned XBE/XEX or container; select a title manifest and pinned upstream toolchain; generate code locally when upstream requires it; build or import the upstream runtime; retain original assets and saves; resolve later upstream releases without replacing the runtime through a second owner. A shared capability should describe these inputs and the upstream recipe rather than absorb another per-game renderer or engine.

Do not merge xboxrecomp and XboxXBERecomp into a fictitious interchangeable engine. Their lifters, runtime interfaces, XDK/signature rules, graphics backends and packaging differ. XenonRecomp explicitly provides no runtime, while ReXGlue provides a full service/runtime layer. A tag, lock file or source checksum supports reproducibility provenance; no pair of clean builds or bit-for-bit output comparison was executed here. Output redistribution cannot be inferred from the compiler or framework license: some upstreams intentionally compile game-derived code only on the player’s machine.

For normal availability-first listings, use the upstream release/importer or honest supported source-build instructions. Preserve an upstream external launcher’s replacement/update ownership, such as Skullburglar. Treat bundled compiler installation, Microsoft library acquisition and game-data preparation as upstream route requirements; this research executed none of them.

## Discovery consequences

Use separate explicit XenonRecomp, ReXGlue, xboxrecomp and hyphenated XboxXBE-Recomp source families. Topic scans and known-framework downstream lists add leads that generic names miss. This pass found 40 missing snapshot leads with those focused routes, including several high-signal projects created or updated close to the audit. Framework demonstration repositories and candidate-game wish lists must not automatically become game records.

Reconcile by immutable repository ID plus current path/redirect and explicit implementation lineage. Store aliases without splitting on owner transfers, new launchers, OS support or channels. Deduplicate a framework page and the same direct upstream before canonical writes; preserve separate games in one runtime and separate independently maintained runtimes of one game. A frozen second reconciliation should create zero records.

Incremental checkpoints should record successful page coverage, stable repo identity and reliable change clocks; a metadata-only recent push is a revisit lead, not proof of new gameplay. Do not advance a completeness checkpoint across failed pages, truncated search, rate failure or unfinished interpretation. Retain a periodic full-source overlap, and never delete canonical identity because a source/README temporarily disappears.

A deterministic burst signal can count distinct credible repository identities first observed within a bounded recent interval for one source-console/framework/topic. Emit a focused reconciliation recommendation with the counted IDs, source clocks and threshold. Count the same game/repository in multiple sources once; distinguish confirmed framework provenance from name/topic hints; do not automatically create game issues from the burst alone.

Dispositions below are **recommendations at the captured baseline**, not claims that root has already created an issue/listing. `New canonical intake` means a legitimate implementation is eligible for normal intake after fresh duplicate/lineage readback. `Research/watch` preserves a concrete milestone rather than claiming supported gameplay. Artwork is never a gate to canonical identity.

### 1. Shin Megami Tensei NINE — SMT NINE Recomp

- Ecosystem: Xbox.
- Upstream: [gymzatan/smt-nine-recomp](https://github.com/gymzatan/smt-nine-recomp); immutable repository ID `1422970813`.
- Architecture/toolchain: Static recompilation / xboxrecomp.
- Disposition: **New canonical intake**; listing eligible: `true`.
- Existing canonical matches at baseline: `[]`. Listing identity: `root intake/reconciliation pending`.
- Edition / source requirements: Japanese retail Xbox disc; ISO/XISO or extracted default.xbe + Media; default.xbe SHA256 6c249401369ed41c2a319762f0b56b80aefde3efe766ac0d8731889a4150deac, complete 3515-file manifest. Chinese optional; English separately patched ISO imported after initial Japanese build.
- Acquisition/build route: Source build setup.cmd/build.cmd; Windows Python3.14 + VS2022 + Rustup. Release assets are updates for an existing installation, not initial game packages.
- Upstream maturity / limitations: Upstream exercised startup, save/load, movies, streets/shops, story battles, RTS tutorials and chapter2 entry. Explicitly no full-playthrough/stable60 guarantee.
- Host platform claims under review: `windows`. These are not Portcove qualification.
- Artwork: Upstream project art linked in primary README where present; root intake must capture through normal artwork pipeline; no art mutation in this research.
- Remaining blocker / next action: Retain source-build discovery; verify update-only asset semantics before adding acquisition button.

## Explicit continuation and validation

The 767 unadjudicated repository leads are enumerated, with source provenance, immutable IDs, primary blob references and a concrete interpretation step, in `coverage-v1.json` as `continuation-required`. They are not silently excluded and have not been counted as reconciled. Continue by descending primary activity within each source family, comparing title-side runtime lineage before intake; resolve known aliases against all open/closed owners and current Project records first.

The 22 missing README records have preserved 404 evidence and explicit next actions in that same file. Do not infer that their repositories were deleted or that existing owners should be closed. New verified native projects should go through root’s normal issue/Project/artwork/schema pipeline; this researcher did not mutate those authorities.

Research verification performed: JSON parses for all named handoffs, exact repository identity/record-count checks, source URL/blob/time presence checks, and preserved HTTP outcome counts. No native builds, gameplay tests, clean-machine installations, output-determinism tests or Portcove schema/catalog generation were executed by this researcher. Those implementation checks belong to the parent discovery-system/catalog work.

## Final semantic continuation checkpoint

This section supersedes the initial 767-lead continuation count above. All 767 retained continuations now have source-attributable semantic dispositions. Including the directly inspected XenonRecomp compiler source gives **986 unique repository leads** overall. No lead remains silently unreviewed or mislabeled unavailable. This completes semantic lead classification; it does not mean every lead is a legitimate independent game runtime or that canonical intake has already happened.

Frozen follow-up artifacts:

- `semantic-continuations-v2.json` — 767 primary-based classifications; SHA-256 `2334167ae14b3c792a7174884b398015c0861a11ddf0cf69d09b34b6e02176e4`.
- `intake-eight-explicit-v1.json` — eight focused native leads with separate source/maturity/acquisition and exact host architecture fields; SHA-256 `30c7392c607e43751744c9b38a2677bd1a77aac94672b369670650130d4c2355`.
- `public-candidate-supplement-v1.json` — 128 prospective public owner/listing candidates plus two withheld documentation-only records, backed by primary title evidence and 130 bounded source-tree captures; SHA-256 `694856342aee273f201bc4bb7c5db1c1e16cdc6a9092ca2dc11f6002b7f489d1`.
- `full-disposition-register-v3.json` — every unique source lead and its final semantic disposition; SHA-256 `edbd8bd3d1db9febf4cae5896f08135c9ebc13f68325f57b4658ec2d2c924954`.

The 279 broad research/watch classifications are not an automatic public `[Port]` creation set. They include generic title scaffolds, discontinued projects and uncertain lineage; uncertain project existence stays with #2931 or an evident existing canonical parent. The strict supplement separately flags public-owner and listing eligibility, explicitly disables automatic acquisition, and requires fresh canonical/closed-owner/PR reconciliation. Independence is not inferred from a different repository ID.

Missing public source is not rejection: DWG2Recomp documents an exact native alpha release and corresponding source archive; The Beatles: Rock Band has a named native runtime binary/config in its tree; project-eleven publishes source archives and exact build/run steps; Conan has detailed title-native claims and retained level profiling evidence. These can justify game-specific research ownership while source/provenance questions remain. Conversely, one-line Beijing2008 and Call-of-Juarez documentation without identifiable title runtime/source/archive evidence remain audit research until the project existence milestone is met.

Final HTTP count is **321 successful bounded requests**, including 144 after the follow-up’s 150-request allowance, with no retries, failures or truncated trees. The parent retained README404 receipts remain preserved separately. No upstream code or artifacts were executed. Native code paths, title manifests, blob IDs and tree SHA are existence/provenance evidence; they are not build, gameplay, distribution-rights or qualification proof.
